Because you would be saying that all the sutras which state that they exist are sutras which do not mean what they say; sutras which you have to interpret to understand their true meaning. And this too is certainly the case, for the autocommentary to the Twenty Verses states that: In the same way, statements by the victorious Buddha where He says that form and other such doors of perception do exist would be examples of His word that must be interpreted to establish their real meaning; statements that are only spoken figuratively, for the benefit of disciples who might require such explanations."

Remember the case of the three different beings looking at a glass full of something wet and flowing; according to you, the pus and blood was actual pus and blood, and the perception of them by the craving spirit was genuine: his visual consciousness was a valid perception."

    "english": "And yet there is no such problem. No matter how many arguments of this kind you want to present, they are all made from the point of view of denying the existence of external objects. The way these arguments go is as follows. If the color blue were to exist as an external object, then the following would occur when this color appeared directly to a sense perception grasping blue; that is, with such a perception found in the mental stream of one of those who \"only sees this side\" [which is another name for those who have not yet perceived emptiness directly]. When an earlier instance of the perception of blue ends, what actually happens is that it plants a mental seed which eventually grows into a later instance of the same perception of blue, when the seed ripens. Suppose the blue were not just this kind of appearance, but rather an appearance where blue as an outer object were transmitting a likeness of itself and thereby appearing to one's perceptions. Something else then would be happening when the three different beings sit down together and look at the glass full of something wet and flowing. The three different objects would be appearing to them because each of the objects was transmitting a likeness of itself to their perception. All of this would be happening independent of any process where each being's karma planted a mental seed, which later ripened and produced the appearance of the object."

    "english": "Just because we said that about the other case doesn't mean that it's true in every case. If the craving spirit hadn't seen any water in that area in the first place, it wouldn't have made any sense for him to go over in that direction to try to enjoy some of the water. Therefore what happened was that, at first, he saw some water. Later on, he was forced by his karma to stop seeing water and saw only bare, parched earth. Then he had an impression where he thought the water had dried up. The case with the fruit tree is the same. Although at first the craving spirit sees a tree loaded with fruit, later on his karma forces him to stop seeing fruit, and all he sees is bare branches. Then he has an impression where he thinks that the tree has no fruit any more."

# Withheld preparation leads: C05:292–294

Preparation only; no baseline, proposal, semantic approval or erratum. Original
angle readers must not read these leads before their separate drafts freeze.
A primary reader later authoring an original must disclose this preparation.
Independent reconciliation and a separate non-author semantic review remain
mandatory. Source wording and all provenance remain unchanged.

Source4337bytes SHA465837385d3b9416a6a26f24266fee21cd81b87b5598e82ff55710c2cf1bd6dd.
Full Wylie/English context286–300 read across this ongoing campaign, including
the new298–300 continuation. Selected full ACIP and all six original physical
extents are verified. All93 complete queries reopened and compared exactly to
SQLite plus original master/corpus objects:71 initial lexical queries with65
full entry occurrences,10 additional lexical,9 equality parallel and3 direct
C15 queries. All captured HGM gloss variants, provenance and statuses read.
Full raw records remain preserved; this preparation is not a semantic verdict.

## 292: figurative sutras and doors of perception

The conditional de ltar ma yin na has an unglossed full entry53653, while
ma yin13878 and na10080 have positive negation/conditional equivalents. Do not
equate null hgm_gloss with absence of a source counterpart. The consequence
thal7394 explicitly has one would have to say that, but grammatical d3
eligibility and any supplied participant require separate judgment.

skye mched692 is an established technical expression with BOTH doorways through
which perceptions grow and doors of perception as positive HGM variants.
The first and second occurrences take different English forms here. Do not
split the source into invented English derivations merely because the first
equivalent is long. gzugs16759/form and la sogs pa19208/and other such have
positive evidence; inflected la sogs pa'i has no exact row. A gated initial
and still needs the exact source licensor if retained as a lexical head.

The first consequence says do not even exist at all; later quoted content
says do exist. med par14539 has never exist at all and other wider variants.
Preserve negation scope and avoid capturing auxiliaries automatically. yod par
18094 has positive to exist but not every actual predicate form; contextual
source reading is necessary.

drang don8829 is curated as the figurative. dgongs pa can2403 positively has
have to interpret and must be interpreted to establish their real meaning,
alongside true meaning. These entries are evidence for broad wording, not an
automatic license for broad d5 spans. The joint drang don dgongs pa can query
has zero exact rows. The phrase is expanded into multiple English sutra
clauses; avoid assigning three English sutras to two explicit Tibetan mdo
tokens without resolving actual repetition and grammatical ownership.

nyi shu pa5707/Twenty Verses and rang 'grel25205/autocommentary are positive.
bcom ldan 'das4317/victorious Buddha is an attested title. Respect complete
title boundaries and supplied articles; do not invent title-member meanings.
The quotation has statements and He says, while the outer reporting clause
says the autocommentary states that. Source gsungs pa21221 and gsungs21219
have both verbal and nominal variants, but one token must not own unrelated
English reprises. bka'401/word is a distinct token and His is contextual.

ston pa7011 is curated as the Teacher; actual ston7006 has teach/saying.
Do not insert Teacher into the actual teaching-verb relations in this source.
'dul ba'i skye bo9569 is an attested full disciples unit; 'dul ba9564 also has
disciples, while skye bo748 has people/beings and inflected skye bo'i68898 is
unglossed. A full expression may be justified without falsely declaring its
components absent. ched du4544 has for the benefit of as a complete relation.
Keep who might require such explanations connected to its source context or
explicitly unwrapped if distributed; a loose phrase must not hide overcapture.

## 293: the same reading of the original quotation

Opening byas pa la/kho na re/zer na frames an interlocutor's claim. English
In response to this someone might claim is a distributed grammatical frame;
HTG22018 and24971 contain whole claim formulas. Preserve which source token
owns any chosen clause; do not double-count the reported-speech relation.
skyon med869 has no problem and wider There's no problem variants.

phyi rol don11899 supplies outer objects/object; both grub pa'i forms have
no exact entry but grub pa2046 has existing/exists. They are two separate
source relations with different English forms. The first relates to forms
and doors, the second to the quoted drop of pus and blood.

The drang don/dgongs pa can pair now appears as only spoken figuratively and
must be interpreted to establish their true meaning. All the positive wider
variants remain visible. Grammatical versus established-unit boundaries,
supplied only/spoken framing and meaning repetition require independent review.
Do not copy an uncertain292 allocation into293 based only on lexical identity.

The source has multiple don tokens and English has point/meaning/saying.
The don query returns both8617 don and18170 yul via its variant. snga ma4017
has original, while the inflected snga ma'i22615 has last/past. mtshungs15769
includes just the same. Exact source and English occurrence decisions matter.
The final rnag khrag23908/pus and blood permits strictly narrower members
only inside that actual parent; both reciprocal constituent variants remain
acknowledged. thigs pa7427/drop and 'ga' yang22309/any at all relate to even
a drop, while med14511 has the full negative-existence alternatives. Do not
invent Tibetan articles to align the supplied English a.

## 294: river and fruit-tree perception contrasted

The repeated river, tree and wet-glass cases have three separate bltas pa and
de'i tshe occurrences. English has two looks at forms then looking at, with
expanded Let's take/Or consider/Remember frames. Resolve occurrences against
the complete argument, including295's correction. No role is settled by
the source's substring order alone.

chu klung4460 has both river of water and riverbed, so its two source instances
can legitimately differ; bskams pa21948/dry is separate from the riverbed.
'bras bu can89784 exists but is unglossed; 'bras bu13429 includes loaded with
fruit and can4128 also has loaded with. Do not hide these competing positive
scopes. ljon pa22940/tree is separate. The second 'bras bu med pa13452 has
bare as well as no fruit. English limbs is supplied imagery, with no separate
Tibetan limb noun here; uncertainty stays unwrapped rather than being filled.

tshad mar song ba has no exact full row. tshad ma15288 has genuinely/genuine,
valid perception and wider variants; tshad mar24806 has authoritative/validity,
and song ba20922 positively includes so. The three genuine/genuinely so
relations must be distinguished from the two explicit valid perception
phrases. Positive song ba/so does not eliminate the need to check the complete
grammatical phrase, actual source range and whether a split would overclaim.

There are three Tibetan mig shes groups but only two English visual
consciousness phrases: the earlier English phrase is collective for the river
and tree cases, while the final one belongs to the later craving-spirit case.
Do not assign the same collective English occurrence independently to both
earlier Tibetan occurrences. Preserve distributed omissions explicitly.
Likewise one source rnag khrag occurs with two English pus and blood phrases;
dngos gnas pa22572/actual relates to the latter predicate wording. Determine
the actual compound occurrence instead of treating the English reprise as
another Tibetan token. The positive reciprocal blood/pus variants do not
settle this occurrence question.

C05:292 — independent Tibetan-first original by Codex; all alignments PROVISIONAL.
Source origin is actual 129e86afae8431aacfb1ee1cee65d8635dd265b8 through285, not a landing baseline; source roles/full original evidence are in ../evidence/source-roles.json.
33 spans; 20 d5; 0 d7; 3 nulls; 0 errata. Every range and candidate occurrence is recorded in actual-ranges.json; every dictionary unit/member was challenged in dictionary-unit-audit.json.
Challenge first: drang don -> “do not mean what they say” is a contextual predicate at d3; dgongs pa can stays lexical at interpret/interpreted without invented members.
The two mdo occurrences own first/second sutras; third English sutras is a repeated noun within the translation, not a third Tibetan occurrence. Final gsungs pa -> states is before the quotation; inner gsungs pa -> says is inside it.
rnams has plural fusion in doorways; ni is an unlexicalized topic marker; zhes has quotation structure only. de ston pas remains omitted because disciples who might require such explanations distributes its function.
Errata: physical lines2876–2889 confirm the wording. C15:437 matches ACIP/Wylie but differs in English do/did and do-exist; no independence/lineage inference. No new defect filed.
FINAL canonical result: EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; body=5927 bytes; exact input/output/error/exit at native-attempts/01/.

===== reconciled/292/report.md =====
C05:292 — Codex independent reconciliation; PROVISIONAL

The two original angles disagree over English closure, phrase/word depth and reporting structure. Reconciliation keeps both complete skye mched technical renderings, the full licensed la sogs pa/and other such closures, and the complete negative existential at phrase depth. thal/would have to say and de/they are recovered from the Tibetan-first proposal; no participant is invented.

drang don is the established figurative classification; the contextual do not mean what they say stays whole at d5, without speculative members. HGM2403 positively licenses have to interpret and the complete must be interpreted to establish their real meaning. Length does not demote those established compounds. HGM9569 supports tight disciples at d5; the broader recipient clause is left unbanked with its distributed teaching/educability reason. zhes is not retained as an absence null: zhes gsungs pa owns states that at phrase depth, while the inner reporting verb owns says.

Two mdo own the first two English sutras. The third sutras and later figurative restatement are repeated translated content, not apparatus or extra source tokens. rnams and ni are the only nulls, for plural fusion and topic grammar. Two exact head allowances are proposed for w3/w22, each naming la sogs pa; no production allowlist is edited.

C15:437 has the same ACIP/Wylie but 756 versus 752 English characters, with do/did and an additional do. Physical C05 preserves the C05 reading. No independent publication or ingestion lineage is established, and no source defect is filed.

Final: 32 spans; 21 non-null d5; 0 d7; 2 nulls; zero errata. Native generator EXIT=0, stderr 0 bytes. Both complete emitted source columns equal the original spine fields. Spec 5784 bytes SHA256 46156aca8a3af9f223f68328ad490f1f97fd51e75058d2c88163f6c61e307c9d; body 5940 bytes SHA256 c5cd6563fe0ed7e37f4b36bb9d4a8a0cff5353b44f5f9d4d56b78b6cb909843a. ===== reconciled/293/report.md =====
C05:293 — Codex independent reconciliation; PROVISIONAL

Retain the established skyon med/no problem compound with two tighter members, both phyi rol don compounds and their members, and rnag khrag with locally justified pus/blood members. sogs owns the complete licensed and similar gesture (one exact head allowance), excluding the following noun. Full dgongs pa can/must be interpreted to establish their true meaning stays at d5 based on HGM2403; no invented interpret/meaning members are added.

The first grub pa owns existing, followed by du/as immediately before outer objects; the second owns exists followed by the later as outer object. Independent don owns point, then meaning of the original statement. The true meaning occurrence is already inside dgongs pa can. The first explanatory this statement does not acquire the later lung. yod, final saying/meaning frame and the negative indefinite 'ga' remain unbanked because their separate allocation is uncertain. The negative existential med/not exist stays tight at d5; does is unwrapped.

Both original angles preserved occurrence corrections despite initial mechanical passes. The reconciler's first planning helper also incorrectly searched backward for unchanged words, selecting earlier as/meaning candidates. Its native attempt 01 and failed checks remain preserved. Corrected eng_order requires an actual new generator attempt 02, which is final; exact intended ranges now match every canonical placement. No source text was changed. ===== tibetan/294/report.md =====
C05:294 — independent Tibetan-first original by Codex; all alignments PROVISIONAL.
Source origin is actual 129e86afae8431aacfb1ee1cee65d8635dd265b8 through285, not a landing baseline; source roles/full original evidence are in ../evidence/source-roles.json.
45 spans; 26 d5; 8 d7; 2 nulls; 0 errata. Every range and candidate occurrence is recorded in actual-ranges.json; every dictionary unit/member was challenged in dictionary-unit-audit.json.
Challenge first: fruitless-tree phrase -> “nothing but bare limbs” is d3 only; no fruit -> limbs lexical pair. can -> loaded with has direct HGM glossary support.
First/second mig shes -> first/second sees; final mig shes -> second visual consciousness. tshad mar/ma occurrences resolve to genuinely, genuinely, valid perception, genuine, valid perception; exact offsets are explicit.
One rnag khrag owns the first pus and blood; the second English repetition stays unwrapped. kyis/gyis nulls are active-subject syntax fusion only. Temporal clauses and song ba remain omitted as distributed.
Errata: physical lines2902–2915 and C15:439 confirm wording. No new defect; field identity establishes no publication/ingestion lineage.
FINAL canonical result: EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; body=6979 bytes; exact input/output/error/exit at native-attempts/01/.
body SHA256 8577984847b9072772ab1000a34e56d27d95755de2edf7d51a0ab9f50f6db7ca; spec SHA256 ed31f113844eaa0be4e171880dc826fbe7d9030942fd986d966127cfc2289239.

===== reconciled/294/report.md =====
C05:294 — Codex independent reconciliation; PROVISIONAL

The fruit-tree discrepancy is resolved with the tight, positive HGM13452 'bras bu med pa/bare at d5. Neither the broad nothing but bare limbs phrase nor complete omission is retained. Limbs and nothing but remain unwrapped; there is no fruit-to-limbs member. The fruit-bearing compound retains separately supported fruit and loaded with members.

The first and second mig shes own the first and second sees, respectively, an attested verbalization (HGM14365) appropriate to the separate river/tree clauses. The collective visual consciousness of both is a repeated summary and is not separately banked. Final mig shes owns the later visual consciousness[598,618] and its genuinely tighter visual/consciousness members. One rnag khrag owns the first pus and blood; the repeated predicate noun stays unwrapped, while dngos gnas pa owns actual.

All three tshad mar song ba validity-result constructions are retained whole at d3: genuinely so twice, then genuine. Positive song ba/so is acknowledged without forcing independent lexical pieces. Two tshad ma/valid perception conclusions remain d5. thal/must and final phyir/Why are recovered. Temporal clauses, possessors and remaining copular/validity frames remain unbanked with specific reasons; the two nulls are active-subject kyis/gyis case fusion.

C15:439 matches ACIP/Wylie/English. P7:35 matches only the first 624 of 642 English characters and ends before valid perception; it cannot corroborate that ending. The physical C05 source does contain it. No publication independence or ingestion lineage is inferred.

Final: 45 spans; 24 non-null d5; 8 d7; 2 nulls; zero errata. Native generator EXIT=0, stderr 0 bytes. Both complete emitted source columns equal the original spine fields. Spec 7244 bytes SHA256 0ac904a42983a9fc4698a00beb1d8e2f989c50139bcf4f02be22e181f41246e5; body 7308 bytes SHA256 7784f85859e76a8cf420064216a33dc720c94f3e765373b5342c13f835a702fa. Actual native inputs/outputs/command/exit: native-attempts/01/.

Every original tuple and its occurrence ranges has a disposition in original-dispositions.json; final-span-decisions.json, resolved-spans.json and material-omissions.json provide all final decisions and residual intervals. Complete original-source evidence is shared once in ../evidence; unchanged frozen original native proofs are hash-verified and cited, with substantive prior attempts retained. All seven source suspicions and their five grounds are in ../source-suspicion-five-ground-checks.json. Source/governing origin is immutable 129e86af through285, not an accepted landing baseline. Mechanical fidelity is established; a separate fresh semantic review must decide acceptance. No hgm_gloss, repository, source, desktop or device state was changed by this role.
